# Skills · D · part 129

869 entries, diffusion-mri to digital-ic. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **diffusion-mri**: Read before fitting, converting or reporting diffusion-weighted MRI: ADC, DTI/DKI, IVIM, NODDI, compartment or spectrum fractions, tractography and diffusion biomarker studies. Prevents silent errors in b-vector frames and vendor vector files, the b-range for ADC/DTI, units, noise floor and uncalibrated or unstable fraction models. Ships tested numpy helpers (frame conversion, tensor fit, noise floor, calibration, stratified recovery and retest tests). Starts from reconstructed DWI. ([KeWang0622/MRFoundry](https://github.com/KeWang0622/MRFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/diffusion-mri) · ★33 · 2 copies)
- **diffusion-mri-superresolution**: Use when low-resolution multidimensional diffusion MRI volumes (e.g., 41×46×28 MUDI images with 1344 diffusion directions) must be upsampled to a higher target grid (e.g., 82×92×56), or when comparing GAN-based superresolution against 3D Unet/3D cGAN baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/diffusion-mri-superresolution))
